DROP TABLE ReturnItem CASCADE CONSTRAINTS;
DROP TABLE Refund CASCADE CONSTRAINTS;
DROP TABLE Pickup CASCADE CONSTRAINTS;
DROP TABLE Payment CASCADE CONSTRAINTS;
DROP TABLE Delivery CASCADE CONSTRAINTS;
DROP TABLE DeliveryService CASCADE CONSTRAINTS;
DROP TABLE OrderDetail CASCADE CONSTRAINTS;
DROP TABLE PointRedemption CASCADE CONSTRAINTS;
DROP TABLE CustomerOrder CASCADE CONSTRAINTS;
DROP TABLE Voucher CASCADE CONSTRAINTS;
DROP TABLE PromotionItem CASCADE CONSTRAINTS;
DROP TABLE Promotion CASCADE CONSTRAINTS;
DROP TABLE StockLog CASCADE CONSTRAINTS;
DROP TABLE PurchaseOrderItem CASCADE CONSTRAINTS;
DROP TABLE PurchaseOrder CASCADE CONSTRAINTS;
DROP TABLE StockBatch CASCADE CONSTRAINTS;
DROP TABLE Stock CASCADE CONSTRAINTS;
DROP TABLE Employee CASCADE CONSTRAINTS;
DROP TABLE Branch CASCADE CONSTRAINTS;
DROP TABLE Supplier CASCADE CONSTRAINTS;
DROP TABLE Item CASCADE CONSTRAINTS;
DROP TABLE Member CASCADE CONSTRAINTS;


CREATE TABLE Member (
    MemberID            NUMBER(6)        NOT NULL,
    Name                VARCHAR2(100)    NOT NULL,
    Email               VARCHAR2(100)    NOT NULL,
    Password            VARCHAR2(255)    NOT NULL,
    PhoneNo             VARCHAR2(20),
    MembershipType      VARCHAR2(20)     NOT NULL,
    MemberPoint         NUMBER(8)        DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL,
    JoinDate             DATE             DEFAULT SYSDATE NOT NULL,
    ExpiryDate           DATE,
    MemberStatus        VARCHAR2(20)     DEFAULT 'Active' NOT NULL,
    DeliveryAddress     VARCHAR2(200),
    CONSTRAINT pk_member PRIMARY KEY (MemberID),
    CONSTRAINT uq_member_email UNIQUE (Email),
    CONSTRAINT ck_member_points CHECK (MemberPoint >= 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_member_type CHECK (MembershipType IN ('Normal','VIP')),
    CONSTRAINT ck_member_status CHECK (MemberStatus IN ('Active','Inactive'))
);


CREATE TABLE Branch (
    BranchID            NUMBER(4)        NOT NULL,
    BranchName          VARCHAR2(100)    NOT NULL,
    Address             VARCHAR2(200)    NOT NULL,
    City                VARCHAR2(50)     NOT NULL,
    State               VARCHAR2(50)     NOT NULL,
    PostCode            VARCHAR2(10),
    BranchEmail         VARCHAR2(100),
    BranchPhoneNo       VARCHAR2(20),
    BranchStatus        VARCHAR2(20)     DEFAULT 'Active' NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT pk_branch PRIMARY KEY (BranchID),
    CONSTRAINT ck_branch_status CHECK (BranchStatus IN ('Active','Inactive'))
);


CREATE TABLE Employee (
    EmployeeID          NUMBER(6)        NOT NULL,
    EmployeeName        VARCHAR2(100)    NOT NULL,
    PhoneNo             VARCHAR2(20),
    Email               VARCHAR2(100),
    Position            VARCHAR2(30)     NOT NULL,
    Salary              NUMBER(10,2),
    EmployeeStatus      VARCHAR2(20)     DEFAULT 'Active' NOT NULL,
    BranchID            NUMBER(4)        NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT pk_employee PRIMARY KEY (EmployeeID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_employee_branch FOREIGN KEY (BranchID)
        REFERENCES Branch(BranchID),
    CONSTRAINT ck_employee_salary CHECK (Salary >= 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_employee_status CHECK (EmployeeStatus IN ('Active','Inactive'))
);


CREATE TABLE Item (
    ItemID              NUMBER(6)        NOT NULL,
    ItemName            VARCHAR2(100)    NOT NULL,
    Brand               VARCHAR2(50),
    Barcode             VARCHAR2(50),
    Unit                VARCHAR2(20)     NOT NULL,
    Price               NUMBER(10,2)     NOT NULL,
    Weight              NUMBER(8,2),
    ItemStatus          VARCHAR2(20)     DEFAULT 'Available' NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT pk_item PRIMARY KEY (ItemID),
    CONSTRAINT uq_item_barcode UNIQUE (Barcode),
    CONSTRAINT ck_item_price CHECK (Price > 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_item_status CHECK (ItemStatus IN ('Available','Unavailable'))
);


CREATE TABLE Stock (
    BranchID            NUMBER(4)        NOT NULL,
    ItemID              NUMBER(6)        NOT NULL,
    Quantity            NUMBER(8)        DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL,
    ReorderLevel        NUMBER(6)        DEFAULT 10 NOT NULL,
    MaximumStock        NUMBER(8)        NOT NULL,
    ShelfLocation       VARCHAR2(50),
    LastUpdated         DATE             DEFAULT SYSDATE NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT pk_stock PRIMARY KEY (BranchID, ItemID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_stock_branch FOREIGN KEY (BranchID)
        REFERENCES Branch(BranchID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_stock_item FOREIGN KEY (ItemID)
        REFERENCES Item(ItemID),
    CONSTRAINT ck_stock_quantity CHECK (Quantity >= 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_stock_reorder CHECK (ReorderLevel >= 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_stock_maximum CHECK (MaximumStock >= 0)
);


CREATE TABLE StockBatch (
    BatchID             NUMBER(8)        NOT NULL,
    Quantity            NUMBER(8)        NOT NULL,
    ExpiryDate          DATE,
    ReceivedDate        DATE             DEFAULT SYSDATE NOT NULL,
    BranchID            NUMBER(4)        NOT NULL,
    ItemID              NUMBER(6)        NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT pk_stock_batch PRIMARY KEY (BatchID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_batch_branch FOREIGN KEY (BranchID)
        REFERENCES Branch(BranchID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_batch_item FOREIGN KEY (ItemID)
        REFERENCES Item(ItemID),
    CONSTRAINT ck_batch_quantity CHECK (Quantity > 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_batch_expiry CHECK (ExpiryDate IS NULL OR ExpiryDate >= ReceivedDate)
);


CREATE TABLE Supplier (
    SupplierID          NUMBER(6)        NOT NULL,
    CompanyName         VARCHAR2(100)    NOT NULL,
    PhoneNo             VARCHAR2(20),
    Email               VARCHAR2(100),
    Address             VARCHAR2(200),
    City                VARCHAR2(50),
    State               VARCHAR2(50),
    PostCode            VARCHAR2(10),
    SupplierStatus      VARCHAR2(20)     DEFAULT 'Active' NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT pk_supplier PRIMARY KEY (SupplierID),
    CONSTRAINT ck_supplier_status CHECK (SupplierStatus IN ('Active','Inactive'))
);


CREATE TABLE PurchaseOrder (
    PurchaseOrderID     NUMBER(8)        NOT NULL,
    OrderDate           DATE             DEFAULT SYSDATE NOT NULL,
    Status              VARCHAR2(20)     DEFAULT 'Pending' NOT NULL,
    SupplierID          NUMBER(6)        NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT pk_purchase_order PRIMARY KEY (PurchaseOrderID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_po_supplier FOREIGN KEY (SupplierID)
        REFERENCES Supplier(SupplierID),
    CONSTRAINT ck_po_status CHECK (Status IN ('Pending','Approved','Received','Cancelled'))
);


CREATE TABLE PurchaseOrderItem (
    PurchaseOrderID     NUMBER(8)        NOT NULL,
    ItemID              NUMBER(6)        NOT NULL,
    Quantity            NUMBER(8)        NOT NULL,
    CostPrice           NUMBER(10,2)     NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT pk_purchase_order_item PRIMARY KEY (PurchaseOrderID, ItemID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_poi_order FOREIGN KEY (PurchaseOrderID)
        REFERENCES PurchaseOrder(PurchaseOrderID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_poi_item FOREIGN KEY (ItemID)
        REFERENCES Item(ItemID),
    CONSTRAINT ck_poi_quantity CHECK (Quantity > 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_poi_cost CHECK (CostPrice > 0)
);


CREATE TABLE StockLog (
    StockLogID          NUMBER(8)        NOT NULL,
    AdjustmentType      VARCHAR2(30)     NOT NULL,
    QuantityChanged     NUMBER(8)        NOT NULL,
    AdjustmentDate      DATE             DEFAULT SYSDATE NOT NULL,
    Remarks             VARCHAR2(300),
    BranchID            NUMBER(4)        NOT NULL,
    ItemID              NUMBER(6)        NOT NULL,
    EmployeeID          NUMBER(6)        NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT pk_stock_log PRIMARY KEY (StockLogID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_stocklog_branch FOREIGN KEY (BranchID)
        REFERENCES Branch(BranchID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_stocklog_item FOREIGN KEY (ItemID)
        REFERENCES Item(ItemID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_stocklog_employee FOREIGN KEY (EmployeeID)
        REFERENCES Employee(EmployeeID)
);


CREATE TABLE Promotion (
    PromotionID         NUMBER(6)        NOT NULL,
    DiscountAmount      NUMBER(10,2)     NOT NULL,
    StartDate           DATE             NOT NULL,
    EndDate             DATE             NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT pk_promotion PRIMARY KEY (PromotionID),
    CONSTRAINT ck_promotion_discount CHECK (DiscountAmount >= 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_promotion_date CHECK (EndDate > StartDate)
);


CREATE TABLE PromotionItem (
    PromotionID         NUMBER(6)        NOT NULL,
    ItemID              NUMBER(6)        NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT pk_promotion_item PRIMARY KEY (PromotionID, ItemID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_promotionitem_promotion FOREIGN KEY (PromotionID)
        REFERENCES Promotion(PromotionID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_promotionitem_item FOREIGN KEY (ItemID)
        REFERENCES Item(ItemID)
);


CREATE TABLE Voucher (
    VoucherID           NUMBER(6)        NOT NULL,
    VoucherName         VARCHAR2(100)    NOT NULL,
    VoucherType         VARCHAR2(20)     NOT NULL,
    DiscountValue       NUMBER(10,2)     NOT NULL,
    MinimumSpend        NUMBER(10,2)     DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL,
    RequiredPoint       NUMBER(8)        DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL,
    StartDate           DATE             NOT NULL,
    ExpiryDate          DATE             NOT NULL,
    VoucherStatus       VARCHAR2(20)     DEFAULT 'Active' NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT pk_voucher PRIMARY KEY (VoucherID),
    CONSTRAINT ck_voucher_discount CHECK (DiscountValue >= 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_voucher_spend CHECK (MinimumSpend >= 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_voucher_point CHECK (RequiredPoint >= 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_voucher_date CHECK (ExpiryDate > StartDate),
    CONSTRAINT ck_voucher_status CHECK (VoucherStatus IN ('Active','Inactive','Expired'))
);


CREATE TABLE CustomerOrder (
    OrderID             NUMBER(8)        NOT NULL,
    OrderDate           DATE             DEFAULT SYSDATE NOT NULL,
    OrderTime           VARCHAR2(10),
    OrderStatus         VARCHAR2(20)     DEFAULT 'Pending' NOT NULL,
    MemberID            NUMBER(6)        NOT NULL,
    BranchID            NUMBER(4)        NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT pk_order PRIMARY KEY (OrderID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_order_member FOREIGN KEY (MemberID)
        REFERENCES Member(MemberID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_order_branch FOREIGN KEY (BranchID)
        REFERENCES Branch(BranchID),
    CONSTRAINT ck_order_status CHECK (OrderStatus IN ('Pending','Completed','Cancelled'))
);


CREATE TABLE OrderDetail (
    ItemID              NUMBER(6)        NOT NULL,
    OrderID             NUMBER(8)        NOT NULL,
    Quantity            NUMBER(6)        NOT NULL,
    UnitPrice           NUMBER(10,2)     NOT NULL,
    Discount            NUMBER(10,2)     DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL,
    LineStatus          VARCHAR2(20)     DEFAULT 'Active' NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT pk_order_detail PRIMARY KEY (ItemID, OrderID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_orderdetail_item FOREIGN KEY (ItemID)
        REFERENCES Item(ItemID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_orderdetail_order FOREIGN KEY (OrderID)
        REFERENCES CustomerOrder(OrderID),
    CONSTRAINT ck_orderdetail_quantity CHECK (Quantity > 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_orderdetail_price CHECK (UnitPrice > 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_orderdetail_discount CHECK (Discount >= 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_orderdetail_status CHECK (LineStatus IN ('Active','Cancelled','Returned'))
);


CREATE TABLE DeliveryService (
    DeliveryServiceID   NUMBER(6)        NOT NULL,
    CompanyName         VARCHAR2(100)    NOT NULL,
    ContactNo           VARCHAR2(20),
    Email               VARCHAR2(100),
    DeliveryCharge      NUMBER(10,2)     DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL,
    CompanyStatus       VARCHAR2(20)     DEFAULT 'Active' NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT pk_delivery_service PRIMARY KEY (DeliveryServiceID),
    CONSTRAINT ck_delivery_charge CHECK (DeliveryCharge >= 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_delivery_company_status CHECK (CompanyStatus IN ('Active','Inactive'))
);


CREATE TABLE Delivery (
    DeliveryID          NUMBER(8)        NOT NULL,
    DeliveryDate        DATE,
    DeliveryTime        VARCHAR2(10),
    DeliveryStatus      VARCHAR2(20)     DEFAULT 'Pending' NOT NULL,
    TrackingNumber      VARCHAR2(100),
    DeliveryRemark      VARCHAR2(300),
    DeliveryServiceID   NUMBER(6)        NOT NULL,
    OrderID             NUMBER(8)        NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT pk_delivery PRIMARY KEY (DeliveryID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_delivery_service FOREIGN KEY (DeliveryServiceID)
        REFERENCES DeliveryService(DeliveryServiceID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_delivery_order FOREIGN KEY (OrderID)
        REFERENCES CustomerOrder(OrderID),
    CONSTRAINT uq_delivery_tracking UNIQUE (TrackingNumber),
    CONSTRAINT uq_delivery_order UNIQUE (OrderID),
    CONSTRAINT ck_delivery_status CHECK (DeliveryStatus IN ('Pending','In Transit','Delivered','Cancelled'))
);


CREATE TABLE Pickup (
    PickupID            NUMBER(8)        NOT NULL,
    PickupDate          DATE,
    PickupTime          VARCHAR2(10),
    PickupCode          VARCHAR2(50),
    PickupAddress       VARCHAR2(200),
    PickupStatus        VARCHAR2(20)     DEFAULT 'Pending' NOT NULL,
    Remarks             VARCHAR2(300),
    OrderID             NUMBER(8)        NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT pk_pickup PRIMARY KEY (PickupID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_pickup_order FOREIGN KEY (OrderID)
        REFERENCES CustomerOrder(OrderID),
    CONSTRAINT uq_pickup_code UNIQUE (PickupCode),
    CONSTRAINT uq_pickup_order UNIQUE (OrderID),
    CONSTRAINT ck_pickup_status CHECK (PickupStatus IN ('Pending','Ready','Collected','Cancelled'))
);


CREATE TABLE Payment (
    PaymentID           NUMBER(8)        NOT NULL,
    PaymentMethod       VARCHAR2(20)     NOT NULL,
    PaymentDate         DATE             DEFAULT SYSDATE NOT NULL,
    AmountPaid          NUMBER(10,2)     NOT NULL,
    TransactionNo       VARCHAR2(100),
    PaymentStatus       VARCHAR2(20)     DEFAULT 'Pending' NOT NULL,
    OrderID             NUMBER(8)        NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT pk_payment PRIMARY KEY (PaymentID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_payment_order FOREIGN KEY (OrderID)
        REFERENCES CustomerOrder(OrderID),
    CONSTRAINT uq_payment_transaction UNIQUE (TransactionNo),
    CONSTRAINT ck_payment_method CHECK (PaymentMethod IN ('Cash','Card','E-Wallet','Online Banking')),
    CONSTRAINT ck_payment_amount CHECK (AmountPaid >= 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_payment_status CHECK (PaymentStatus IN ('Pending','Paid','Failed','Refunded'))
);


CREATE TABLE PointRedemption (
    PointRedemptionID   NUMBER(8)        NOT NULL,
    RedemptionDate      DATE             DEFAULT SYSDATE NOT NULL,
    PointUsed           NUMBER(8)        NOT NULL,
    RedemptionStatus    VARCHAR2(20)     DEFAULT 'Pending' NOT NULL,
    Remarks             VARCHAR2(300),
    MemberID            NUMBER(6)        NOT NULL,
    VoucherID           NUMBER(6)        NOT NULL,
    OrderID             NUMBER(8)        NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT pk_point_redemption PRIMARY KEY (PointRedemptionID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_redemption_member FOREIGN KEY (MemberID)
        REFERENCES Member(MemberID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_redemption_voucher FOREIGN KEY (VoucherID)
        REFERENCES Voucher(VoucherID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_redemption_order FOREIGN KEY (OrderID)
        REFERENCES CustomerOrder(OrderID),
    CONSTRAINT ck_redemption_points CHECK (PointUsed > 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_redemption_status CHECK (RedemptionStatus IN ('Pending','Completed','Cancelled'))
);


CREATE TABLE Refund (
    RefundID            NUMBER(8)        NOT NULL,
    RefundDate          DATE             DEFAULT SYSDATE NOT NULL,
    RefundReason        VARCHAR2(300)    NOT NULL,
    ReturnMethod        VARCHAR2(30)     NOT NULL,
    EvidencePhoto       VARCHAR2(300),
    RefundAmount        NUMBER(10,2)     NOT NULL,
    RefundRemark        VARCHAR2(300),
    RefundStatus        VARCHAR2(20)     DEFAULT 'Pending' NOT NULL,
    OrderID             NUMBER(8)        NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT pk_refund PRIMARY KEY (RefundID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_refund_order FOREIGN KEY (OrderID)
        REFERENCES CustomerOrder(OrderID),
    CONSTRAINT ck_refund_amount CHECK (RefundAmount >= 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_refund_status CHECK (RefundStatus IN ('Pending','Approved','Rejected','Completed'))
);


CREATE TABLE ReturnItem (
    RefundID            NUMBER(8)        NOT NULL,
    ItemID              NUMBER(6)        NOT NULL,
    Quantity            NUMBER(6)        NOT NULL,
    ItemCondition       VARCHAR2(30)     NOT NULL,
    ReturnRemark        VARCHAR2(300),
    CONSTRAINT pk_return_item PRIMARY KEY (RefundID, ItemID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_returnitem_refund FOREIGN KEY (RefundID)
        REFERENCES Refund(RefundID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_returnitem_item FOREIGN KEY (ItemID)
        REFERENCES Item(ItemID),
    CONSTRAINT ck_returnitem_quantity CHECK (Quantity > 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_returnitem_condition CHECK
        (ItemCondition IN ('New','Good','Damaged','Defective'))
);
